Subaru shares many parts across engines, markets and models. Compatibility is assessed per part using OEM numbers, documented replacements, manufacturer applications, dimensions and installed-component evidence. Catalog inclusion is a starting point, not confirmation. Check production-date and equipment restrictions.
Catalog rows are unreviewed. Candidate relationships are not verified supersessions. Unknown build date yields conditional fitment.
